
==========================================================================================
SHEET: README
==========================================================================================
  0: Item | Details
  1: Workbook name | Intelligent Workflow Manager — Complete Dashboard Use Case
  2: Purpose | A complete pharmacovigilance workflow-management use case.
  3: Dashboard as-of date | Live Data of the LSMV 
  4: Case population | 165 fully populated sample cases
  5: Priority scenario | 12 high regulatory-risk cases, 5 delayed Medical Review activities, Person A at 125% capacity, and 3 significantly aged activities.
  6: Business objective | Improved PV operational efficiency; stronger management oversight; and audit readiness.
  7: Control-tower purpose | The dashboard consolidates workflow health, regulatory and SLA risk, predictive signals, bottlenecks, capacity, critical cases, aging activities, and management actions.
  8: Data relationships | Case ID connects Dashboard_Case_Detail, Workflow_Activities, Assignment_History, and Risk_Recommendations. Activity ID enables activity-level drill-down.
  9: Suggested review order | README → Business_Value → Expected_Dashboard_Outcomes → Sample_Dashboard_Snapshot → Dashboard_Case_Detail → supporting tables.

==========================================================================================
SHEET: Business_Value
==========================================================================================
  0: Business Value Area | Use Case Outcome
  1: Improved PV operational efficiency | A single control-tower view replaces manual review of multiple QBE/LSBI reports; priority cases, due dates, owners, and next actions are visible together.
  2: Stronger management oversight | Managers can monitor overall workflow health, deadline pressure, bottlenecks, workload imbalance, aging activities, and repeated reassignment from one dashboard.
  3: Audit readiness | Every P1 action contains due-date evidence, historical comparison, risk reason, generated timestamp, action status, audit reference, and human approval control.
  4: Traditional reporting | Shows what happened in prior reports.
  5: Intelligent Workflow Manager | Shows what is happening, why it is happening, what is likely to happen next, and what management action should be considered.
  6: Differentiation | Combines real-time workflow health, predictive regulatory and SLA risk, bottleneck intelligence, workload intelligence, explainability, and management actions in one PV control tower.

==========================================================================================
SHEET: Expected_Dashboard_Outcomes
==========================================================================================
  0: Acceptance ID | Acceptance Criterion | Validation Method | Expected Dashboard Outcome | Supporting Tabs
  1: AC-01 | Workflow Health | Overall health score and status are shown in Sample_Dashboard_Snapshot. | 62/100, Amber - Attention Required, with contributing metrics visible. | Sample_Dashboard_Snapshot
  2: AC-02 | Regulatory Risk | Filter Regulatory Risk = High in Dashboard_Case_Detail. | 12 cases are identified before their Submission Due Date becomes overdue. | Dashboard_Case_Detail; Risk_Recommendations
  3: AC-03 | Predictive Risk | Filter Predictive SLA Risk = High and review reason/evidence. | 12 potential SLA-breach cases are identified using historical workflow performance. | Dashboard_Case_Detail; Historical_Benchmarks; Risk_Recommendations
  4: AC-04 | Bottleneck Detection | Open Workflow_Bottlenecks and select Medical Review. | Medical Review is Critical with 5 active breached activities. | Workflow_Bottlenecks; Workflow_Activities
  5: AC-05 | Workload Intelligence | Open User_Capacity and select Person A. | Person A is highlighted at 125% capacity utilization. | User_Capacity; Dashboard_Case_Detail
  6: AC-06 | Explainability | Open any P1 record in Risk_Recommendations. | Risk reason, supporting data, historical comparison, dates, and owner are visible. | Risk_Recommendations; Dashboard_Case_Detail
  7: AC-07 | Next Best Action | Review Recommended Action for a P1 case, bottleneck, or capacity constraint. | Appropriate action is provided: prioritize, reassign, escalate, or monitor. | Risk_Recommendations; Workflow_Bottlenecks; User_Capacity
  8: AC-08 | Drill-down | Navigate dashboard → workflow → team → case → activity using Case ID. | All levels are linked through Receipt#, Activity ID, Processing Unit, and owner. | Sample_Dashboard_Snapshot; Dashboard_Case_Detail; Workflow_Activities; Assignment_History
  9: AC-09 | Agentic Interaction | Use the questions in Example_Manager_Questions. | Questions on risk, bottlenecks, capacity, aged work, and reassignment return linked evidence. | Example_Manager_Questions; all supporting tabs
 10: AC-10 | Governance | Review P1 action records. | Each P1 item carries supporting data, generated timestamp, audit reference, action status, and Human Approval Required = Yes. | Risk_Recommendations

==========================================================================================
SHEET: Example_Manager_Questions
==========================================================================================
  0: Example Manager Question | Expected Explainable Response | Primary Data Tables
  1: Which cases are most likely to miss their regulatory deadline? | Return the 12 high regulatory-risk cases ranked by score, with SDD, CDD, current activity, owner, reason, and action. | Dashboard_Case_Detail; Risk_Recommendations
  2: Why is Medical Review currently a bottleneck? | Show five breached Medical Review activities, activity age versus historical benchmark, reviewer capacity, and queue action. | Workflow_Bottlenecks; Workflow_Activities; User_Capacity
  3: Which cases have the highest predicted SLA-breach risk? | Return high predictive-risk cases with probability, activity aging, historical turnaround time, activity due status, CDD buffer, and reason. | Dashboard_Case_Detail; Historical_Benchmarks
  4: Who is operating above capacity? | Return Person A and Person D with planned versus available hours, utilization, active workload, and capacity action. | User_Capacity; Dashboard_Case_Detail
  5: Which cases have been reassigned more than once? | Return reassignment timeline, owners, timestamp, reason, and handover status. | Assignment_History
  6: What requires action today? | Return P1 actions, breached Activity Due Dates, and cases with SDD today or tomorrow. | Sample_Dashboard_Snapshot; Risk_Recommendations; Dashboard_Case_Detail

==========================================================================================
SHEET: Data_Derivation_Rules
==========================================================================================
  0: Field / Measure | Baseline Rule
  1: Reference data style | The supplied 165 records were used as a formatting and vocabulary reference. This workbook is a fully populated sample use case with 165 generated case records.
  2: Case complexity | Large: at least 25 suspect products and 30 events. Medium: at least 10 suspect products and 10 events. Low: 1–9 suspect products and 1–9 events.
  3: Regulatory Risk | High where active Submission Due Date is within 2 calendar days; Medium in the 3–6 day monitoring window; Low when outside monitoring or completed.
  4: SLA Risk | High where active Case Due Date is within 2 calendar days; Medium in the 3–6 day monitoring window; Low when outside monitoring or completed.
  5: Predictive SLA Risk | Uses current activity aging compared with historical turnaround time, Activity Due Status, and Case Due Date buffer. High is 75% or above predicted breach probability.
  6: Bottleneck Status | Critical when a processing unit has two or more active activities with breached Activity Due Date; At Risk for one breach or sustained due-date pressure.
  7: Aging | For active cases: dashboard date minus creation date. For completed cases: current closure activity completion minus creation date.
  8: Historical turnaround time | Stage- and complexity-specific historical median used for explainable comparison in the current workflow activity.
  9: Significantly Longer than Historical Norm | Yes where current active activity aging is at least 2.0x the corresponding historical turnaround time.
 10: Governance | Management actions remain subject to Human Approval Required = Yes. Risk action records store supporting data, timestamp, action status, and audit reference.
